<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>長聲個</rb><rt>dziang-sing-go</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>記念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>仇敵個</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>第二年</rb><rt>Di-nyi-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>二月</rb><rt>nyi-yüih</rt><rb>二十</rb><rt>nyi-jih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>法幕</rb><rt>fah-mo</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>收上去</rb><rt>siu-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拔營</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西難</rb><rt>Si-næn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>停落</rb><rt>ding-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>第一遭</rb><rt>di-ih-tsao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>Di-ih</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>大旗下個</rb><rt>do-gyi-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>統帶個</rb><rt>tʽong-ta-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞米拿達</rb><rt>Üô-mi-nô-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿順</rb><rt>Nô-jing</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>統帶</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以薩迦</rb><rt>Yi-seh-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蘇押</rb><rt>Su-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿但業</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>統帶</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西布倫</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希倫</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-leng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>拆</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>革順</rb><rt>Kah-jing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米拉利</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>搬運</rb><rt>pun-yüing</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>後</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>動身個</rb><rt>dong-sing-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>大旗下個</rb><rt>do-gyi-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做其</rb><rt>tso-gyi</rt><rb>統帶個</rb><rt>tʽong-ta-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>示丟珥</u>個</rb><rt>Z-tiu-r-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利蓿</rb><rt>Yi-li-soh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>統帶</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西緬</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蘇利沙太</rb><rt>Su-li-sô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示路蔑</rb><rt>Z-lu-mih</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>統帶</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>丟珥</rb><rt>Tiu-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利雅薩</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哥轄</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>抬勒</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>聖所</rb><rt>sing-sô</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>未到</rb><rt>vi-tao</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>搭好</rb><rt>tah-hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>動身個</rb><rt>dong-sing-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法蓮</rb><rt>Yi-feh-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>大旗下個</rb><rt>do-gyi-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>統帶個</rb><rt>tʽong-ta-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞米忽</rb><rt>Üô-mi-hwah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙瑪</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-mô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>統帶</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其田尼</rb><rt>Gyi-din-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞比但</rb><rt>Üô-pi-dæn</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>動身個</rb><rt>dong-sing-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但</rb><rt>Dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>大旗下個</rb><rt>do-gyi-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>統帶個</rb><rt>tʽong-ta-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞米沙太</rb><rt>Üô-mi-sô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞歇斯</rb><rt>Üô-hyih-s</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>統帶</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞設</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>惡蘭</rb><rt>Ôh-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帕結</rb><rt>Pʽô-kyih</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>統帶</rb><rt>Tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>納大利</rb><rt>Nah-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以南</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-nen-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞希拉</rb><rt>Üô-hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>各隊兵</rb><rt>kôh-de-ping</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流珥</rb><rt>Liu-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿巴</rb><rt>O-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗去</rb><rt>feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>如何</rb><rt>jü-ʽo</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>眼目</rb><rt>ngæn-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>do-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>法櫃</rb><rt>fah-gwe</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>安息個</rb><rt>en-sih-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>拔營</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>日裏</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>法櫃</rb><rt>Fah-gwe</rt><rb>上去個</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>法櫃</rb><rt>Fah-gwe</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>千千</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsʽin</rt><rb>萬萬</rb><rt>væn-væn</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Ms. XI.

<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>PAH-SING</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>發怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>着來</rb><rt>dziah-læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>燒掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>訴苦</rb><rt>su-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>熓了</rb><rt>u-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>dziah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大比剌</rb><rt>Da-pi-lah</rt></ruby></u>。（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>燒</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>。）

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>外族個</rb><rt>nga-dzoh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>貪喫個</rb><rt>tʽen-kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>號淚</rb><rt>ʽao-li</rt><rb>大哭</rb><rt>da-kʽoh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>黃瓜</rb><rt>wông-kwô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>西瓜</rb><rt>si-kwô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>韭菜</rb><rt>kyiu-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蔥</rb><rt>tsʽong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>大蒜</rb><rt>da-sön</rt></ruby>；

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>精力</rb><rt>tsing-lih</rt><rb>衰敗</rb><rt>sæ-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-liao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>嗎嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>嗎嗱</rb><rt>Mô-nô</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>豆蔻</rb><rt>deo-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顏色</rb><rt>ngæn-seh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>撮勒來</rb><rt>tsʽah-leh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>磨</rb><rt>mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>舂</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>搗臼</rb><rt>tao-gyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>煮</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>鑊裏</rb><rt>ʽoh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>夜裏</rb><rt>Yia-li</rt><rb>露水</rb><rt>lu-shü</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嗎嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>隨勒</rb><rt>ze-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>極其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>發怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>弗好</rb><rt>feh-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>賜恩</rb><rt>s-eng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>擔當</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>責任</rb><rt>tsah-zing</rt></ruby>？

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>懷</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>致使得</rb><rt>cü-s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>懷裏</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>喫奶個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-na-go</rt><rb>嬰孩</rb><rt>ing-ʽæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>列祖個</rb><rt>lih-tsu-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>責任</rb><rt>tsah-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>擔當</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>弗起</rb><rt>feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>tʽông-jün</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>苦難</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>召攏</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>官府個</rb><rt>kwun-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降臨</rb><rt>kông-ling</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>擔當</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>責任</rb><rt>tsah-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>獨立</rb><rt>doh-lih</rt><rb>擔當</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-tô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>哭泣</rb><rt>kʽoh-kyʽih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好好個</rb><rt>hao-hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> 『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗單只</rb><rt>feh-tæn-tsih</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兩日</rb><rt>liang-nyih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>五日</rb><rt>ng-nyih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>十日</rb><rt>jih-nyih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>廿日</rb><rt>nyiæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；

<sup>20</sup> 『<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>Dzih</rt><rb>喫到</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tao</rt><rb>一個月</rb><rt>ih-ko-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甚至於</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>鼻頭管</rb><rt>bih-deo-kwun</rt><rb>噴出來</rb><rt>pʽeng-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>厭憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>住來</rb><rt>djü-læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央個</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>哭泣</rb><rt>kʽoh-kyʽih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？』」

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六十萬</rb><rt>loh-jih-væn</rt><rb>步兵</rb><rt>bu-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一個月</rb><rt>ih-ko-yüih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>全隊</rb><rt>djün-de</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全隊</rb><rt>djün-de</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫飽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>收攏</rb><rt>siu-long</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫飽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>力</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>足</rb><rt>tsoh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>應驗</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應驗</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>召攏</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>雲裏</rb><rt>yüing-li</rt><rb>降臨</rb><rt>kông-ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>七十個</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>靈個</rb><rt>ling-go</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>Gyi-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一名</rb><rt>ih-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利達</rb><rt>Li-deh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一名</rb><rt>ih-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米達</rb><rt>Mi-deh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>營寨裏</rb><rt>ying-dza-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>少年</rb><rt>siao-nyin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奔來</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>利達</rb><rt>Li-deh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>米達</rb><rt>Mi-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>個</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>妒忌</rb><rt>tu-gyi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>願</rb><rt>Nyün</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>營寨裏</rb><rt>ying-dza-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>風</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>發起</rb><rt>fah-kyʽi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>鵪鶉鳥</rb><rt>in-jing-tiao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>約來</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt><rb>之內</rb><rt>ts-nen</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>疊來</rb><rt>diah-læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二尺</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽah</rt><rb>厚</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>整日</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt><rb>整夜</rb><rt>tsing-yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>第二日</rb><rt>di-nyi-nyih</rt><rb>整日</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>鵪鶉鳥</rb><rt>in-jing-tiao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>撮勒</rb><rt>tsʽah-leh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十</rb><rt>jih</rt><rb>賀梅<u>珥</rb><rt>ʽo-me-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>鋪開</rb><rt>pʽu-kʽæ</rt><rb>在營寨</rb><rt>dzæ-ying-dza</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>肉</rb><rt>Nyüoh</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>牙齒裏</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>嚼糊</rb><rt>ziah-wu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>大怒</rb><rt>da-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>私慾</rb><rt>s-yüoh</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>乞律夏大法</rb><rt>Kyʽih-lih-yüô-da-fah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈洗錄</rb><rt>Ha-si-loh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈洗錄</rb><rt>Ha-si-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XII.

<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>MO-SI</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古實</rb><rt>Kwu-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古實</rb><rt>Kwu-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>講過</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>溫柔</rb><rt>w̆eng-ziu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>降臨</rb><rt>kông-ling</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>雲柱</rb><rt>yüing-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>Tsiang-bong</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>若有</rb><rt>ziah-yiu</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>異像</rb><rt>yi-ziang</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>夢裏</rb><rt>mong-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>對口</rb><rt>te-kʽeo</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>隱藏</rb><rt>ing-dzông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>親眼</rb><rt>tsʽing-ngæn</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>發怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>Yüing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>Tsiang-bong</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雪</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt></ruby>；

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>愚笨個</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>孃胎</rb><rt>nyiang-tʽæ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>半身</rb><rt>pun-sing</rt><rb>爛糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>哀求</rb><rt>æ-gyiu</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>求懇</rb><rt>Gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>父親</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>饞唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>唾</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt><rb>關出</rb><rt>kwæn-cʽih</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>營寨裏</rb><rt>ying-dza-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>關出</rb><rt>kwæn-cʽih</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>帶進</rb><rt>ta-tsing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈洗錄</rb><rt>Ha-si-loh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>牧伯個</rb><rt>moh-pah-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>屬</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒格</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-kah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>沙母亞</rb><rt>Sô-meo-üô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差去</rb><rt>tsʽa-kyʽi</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>個</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿書亞</rb><rt>Ah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>望</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>山路</rb><rt>sæn-lu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>剛強</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>住個</rb><rt>djü-go</rt><rb>地段</rb><rt>di-dön</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>堅固個</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>厚實</rb><rt>ʽeo-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>敲掐</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽah</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放大</rb><rt>fông-do</rt><rb>膽子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>地土個</rb><rt>di-tʽu-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>馱點</rb><rt>do-tin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>初</rb><rt>tsʽu</rt><rb>熟</rb><rt>joh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。
