<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汛</rb><rt>Sing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利哈</rb><rt>Li-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈馬</rb><rt>Ha-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>望</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>介去</rb><rt>ka-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯崙</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞衲</rb><rt>Üô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞希幔</rb><rt>Üô-hyi-mæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>示篩</rb><rt>Z-s</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>達買</rb><rt>Dah-mæ</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯崙</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國個</rb><rt>koh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑣安</rb><rt>So-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>建造個</rb><rt>kyin-zao-go</rt><rb>七年</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實各</rb><rt>Yi-jih-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嶴</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>斬落</rb><rt>tsæn-lôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一球</rb><rt>ih-gyiu</rt><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt><rb>結的個</rb><rt>kyih-tih-go</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>槓子</rb><rt>kông-ts</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>石榴</rb><rt>zih-liu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>斬落</rb><rt>tsæn-lôh</rt><rb>一球</rb><rt>ih-gyiu</rt><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實各</rb><rt>Yi-jih-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嶴</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>。（<u><ruby><rb>以實各</rb><rt>Yi-jih-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>翻出來</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>一球</rb><rt>ih-gyiu</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。）

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會個</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野個</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>回報</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-liao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真正</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖個</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>結個</rb><rt>kyih-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>剛強</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞衲</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-neh-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>住來</rb><rt>djü-læ</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Hah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>住來</rb><rt>djü-læ</rt><rb>海邊</rb><rt>hæ-pin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河邊</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>着實</rb><rt>dziah-jih</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>得勝</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去過</rb><rt>kyʽi-ko</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>剛強</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>回報</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窺探個</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吞喫</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>身體</rb><rt>sing-tʽi</rt><rb>長大個</rb><rt>dziang-do-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞衲</rb><rt>Üô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>衲非蓮</rb><rt>Neh-fi-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Ms. XIV.

<ruby><rb>當時</rb><rt>TÔNG-Z</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>吵鬧</rb><rt>tsʽao-nao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇夜</rb><rt>keh-yia</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>號哭</rb><rt>ʽao-kʽoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>死來</rb><rt>si-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>死來</rb><rt>si-læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>倒弗如</rb><rt>tao-feh-jü</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>彼此</rb><rt>pe-tsʽ</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會個</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撲倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>個</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扯開</rb><rt>tsʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>窺探個</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>極其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>肥美</rb><rt>vi-me</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真正</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖個</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>切弗可</rb><rt>tsʽih-feh-kʽo</rt><rb>悖逆</rb><rt>be-nyih</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>吞喫</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>保護</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>輕看</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>剛強</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>此地個</rb><rt>tsʽ-di-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>對面</rb><rt>te-min</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>日裏</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>雲柱</rb><rt>yüing-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夜裏</rb><rt>yia-li</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>火柱</rb><rt>ho-djü</rt><rb>引導</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>別國個</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>16</sup> 『<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>緩緩</rb><rt>wun-wun</rt><rb>發怒個</rb><rt>fah-nu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大慈</rb><rt>da-dz</rt><rb>大悲</rb><rt>da-pe</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>過犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斷</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>父個</rb><rt>vu-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>三四</rb><rt>sæn-s</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>求懇</rb><rt>Gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>慈悲</rb><rt>dz-pe</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永活個</rb><rt>üong-weh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>充滿</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>雖然</rb><rt>se-jün</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>試探</rb><rt>s-tʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>十遭</rb><rt>jih-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>列祖</rb><rt>lih-tsu</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>輕看</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽen</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>跟從</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>去過個</rb><rt>kyʽi-ko-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>住來</rb><rt>djü-læ</rt><rb>山嶴</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>倒走轉</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>紅</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>27</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兇惡</rb><rt>hyüong-oh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽見了</rb><rt>tʽing-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永活個</rb><rt>üong-weh-go</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耳朵裏</rb><rt>ng-tô-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>數個</rb><rt>su-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>總數</rb><rt>tsong-su</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>廿歲</rb><rt>nyiæn-shü</rt><rb>以上</rb><rt>yi-zông</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>Pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶孚尼</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>個</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>進去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>飄流</rb><rt>pʽiao-liu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擔當</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>悖逆個</rb><rt>be-nyih-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>消滅</rb><rt>siao-mih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>箇地個</rb><rt>keh-di-go</rt><rb>日數</rb><rt>nyih-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抵</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>擔當</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兇惡</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差去</rb><rt>tsʽa-kyʽi</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>回報</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>回報</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>弗好</rb><rt>feh-hao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>個</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>極其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>早晨</rb><rt>tsao-jing</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-ting</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>順流</rb><rt>jing-liu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>Feh-iao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免得</rb><rt>min-teh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>打敗</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>竟是介</rb><rt>kying-z-ka</rt><rb>放開</rb><rt>fông-kʽæ</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>膽子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山頂頭</rb><rt>sæn-ting-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>法櫃</rb><rt>fah-gwe</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>庉在</rb><rt>deng-dzæ</rt><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打敗</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>追</rb><rt>tse</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>盍馬</rb><rt>ʽEh-mô</rt></ruby></u>。

### Ms. XV.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>居住</rb><rt>kyü-djü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>獻祭</rb><rt>hyin-tsi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>燒化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還願</rb><rt>wæn-nyün</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心願</rb><rt>sing-nyün</rt><rb>祭個</rb><rt>tsi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>定好個</rb><rt>ding-hao-go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>牛隊</rb><rt>ngeo-de</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊隊</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>噴香</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4, 5</sup> <ruby><rb>獻</rb><rt>Hyin</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燒化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭個</rb><rt>tsi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>四股之一</rb><rt>s-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>四股之一</rb><rt>s-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>麪粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>噴香個</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燒化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>無論</rb><rt>vu-leng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>還願</rb><rt>wæn-nyün</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>謝恩</rb><rt>zia-eng</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>半欣</rb><rt>pun-hying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>半欣</rb><rt>pun-hying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>噴香個</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>燒化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>噴香個</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>樣子</rb><rt>yiang-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>外來</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央個</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>噴香</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央個</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>外來</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>禮儀</rb><rt>li-nyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>麥食</rb><rt>mah-zih</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>搖祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>嘗新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>麥粉</rb><rt>mah-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>搖祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>曬場裏個</rb><rt>sa-dziang-li-go</rt><rb>搖祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>嘗新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>麥粉</rb><rt>mah-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>搖祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>錯犯</rb><rt>tsʽo-væn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>無意</rb><rt>vu-i</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燒化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>噴香個</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奠個</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>定例</rb><rt>ding-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>錯犯個</rb><rt>tsʽo-væn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>錯犯</rb><rt>tsʽo-væn</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>外來</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>錯犯個</rb><rt>tsʽo-væn-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>無意</rb><rt>vu-i</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雌</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>無意</rb><rt>vu-i</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>無意</rb><rt>vu-i</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>外來</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必有</rb><rt>pih-yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>例</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>故意</rb><rt>kwu-i</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>無論</rb><rt>vu-leng</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>外來</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>輕看</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽen</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>柴</rb><rt>za</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>Pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>柴個</rb><rt>za-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會個</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>衦頭</rb><rt>ken-den</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>蘇頭</rb><rt>su-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蘇頭</rb><rt>su-deo</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>藍</rb><rt>læn</rt><rb>線</rb><rt>sin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>S-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>Sing-jün</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XVI.

<ruby><rb><u>利未</u>個</rb><rt>LI-VI-GO</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哥轄</u>個</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh-go</rt><rb>孫子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以士哈</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-z-hah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利押</rb><rt>Yi-li-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞比蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>比拉</u>個</rb><rt>Pi-lah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>En</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>結黨</rb><rt>kyih-tông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>過份</rb><rt>ko-veng</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>個個</rb><rt>ko-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>抬舉</rb><rt>dæ-kyü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>之</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一黨</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>揀選個</rb><rt>kæn-shün-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一黨</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>Ming-tsiao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>燒</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誠心</rb><rt>dzing-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾<u>拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>利</u>未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>過份</rb><rt>ko-veng</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>挑選</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>小事</rb><rt>siao-z</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>近攏來</rb><rt>gying-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>祭司個</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一黨</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？」
