### Ms. XX.

<ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH-GO</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>正月裏</rb><rt>tsing-yüih-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汛</u>個</rb><rt>Sing-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死在</rb><rt>si-dzæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>葬好</rb><rt>tsông-hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>公會</rb><rt>Kong-we</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>從前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>死在</rb><rt>si-dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>！

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上來</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>弗好</rb><rt>feh-hao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>弗好</rb><rt>feh-hao</rt><rb>種田</rb><rt>cong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>石榴</rb><rt>zih-liu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>Do-leh</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聚集</rb><rt>jü-jih</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>悖逆個</rb><rt>Be-nyih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>兩記</rb><rt>liang-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>頂多</rb><rt>ting-to</rt><rb>流出來</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>尊</rb><rt>tseng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>聖善來</rb><rt>sing-jün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利巴</rb><rt>Mi-li-pô</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>翻出來</rb><rt>Fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>爭</rb><rt>Tsang</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。）

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着個</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt><rb>苦難</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>頂兇</rb><rt>ting-hyüong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>哀求</rb><rt>æ-gyiu</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打發</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>境界</rb><rt>kying-ka</rt><rb>邊沿個</rb><rt>pin-yin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葡萄園裏</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>井裏個</rb><rt>tsing-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>大路</rb><rt>do-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>岔到</rb><rt>tsʽô-tao</rt><rb>左邊</rb><rt>tso-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>岔到</rb><rt>tsʽô-tao</rt><rb>右邊</rb><rt>yiu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>大路</rb><rt>do-lu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只要</rb><rt>tsih-iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>步行</rb><rt>bu-ʽang</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>斷</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>率領</rb><rt>sah-ling</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>大力量</rb><rt>do-lih-liang</rt><rb>阻擋</rb><rt>tsu-tông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>24</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米利巴</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-li-pô-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞倫</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>衣服</rb><rt>i-voh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>穿在</rb><rt>cʽün-dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>死在</rb><rt>si-dzæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞倫</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>衣服</rb><rt>i-voh</rt><rb>穿在</rb><rt>cʽün-dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死在</rb><rt>si-dzæ</rt><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全族</rb><rt>djün-dzoh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XXI.

<ruby><rb>住在</rb><rt>DJÜ-DZÆ</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>Nen-pin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞臘</rb><rt>Üô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>聽見</rb><rt>tʽing-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞大林</rb><rt>Üô-da-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一路</rb><rt>ih-lu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>許願</rb><rt>hyü-nyün</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>毀滅</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>毀滅</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>盍馬</rb><rt>ʽEh-mô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>紅</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>邊</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>介走</rb><rt>ka-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>繞轉</rb><rt>nyiao-cün</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以東</u>地</rb><rt>Yi-tong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>銳氣</rb><rt>ze-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>死在</rb><rt>si-dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>厭憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>淡薄個</rb><rt>dæn-boh-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>火蛇</rb><rt>ho-dzô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>咬</rb><rt>ngao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-liao</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>馱過</rb><rt>do-ko</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>製造</rb><rt>cü-zao</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>火蛇</rb><rt>ho-dzô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>掛來</rb><rt>kwô-læ</rt><rb>旗杆</rb><rt>gyi-ken</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>咬個</rb><rt>ngao-go</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>製造</rb><rt>cü-zao</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>銅蛇</rb><rt>dong-dzô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>掛來</rb><rt>kwô-læ</rt><rb>旗杆</rb><rt>gyi-ken</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>咬個</rb><rt>ngao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>銅蛇</rb><rt>dong-dzô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>活了</rb><rt>weh-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿泊</rb><rt>O-boh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿泊</rb><rt>O-boh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以雅巴林</rb><rt>Yi-yüô-pô-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>東邊個</rb><rt>tong-pin-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒烈</rb><rt>Sah-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>邊個</rb><rt>pin-go</rt><rb>嶴</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>境界</rb><rt>kying-ka</rt><rb>流出來</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>流到</rb><rt>liu-tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞摩利</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li-go</rt><rb>交界</rb><rt>kao-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt><rb>書有</rb><rt>shü-yiu</rt><rb>記的</rb><rt>kyi-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>蘇法</u>個</rb><rt>Su-fah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞協</rb><rt>Üô-yiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>邊個</rb><rt>pin-go</rt><rb>嶴</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞珥</rb><rt>Üô-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>流落來</rb><rt>liu-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>境界</rb><rt>kying-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>從前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇口</rb><rt>keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>唱歌</rb><rt>tsʽông-ko</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>井</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>湧出</rb><rt>üong-cʽih</rt><rb>好水</rb><rt>hao-shü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歌唱</rb><rt>ko-tsʽông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>井</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>圭</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>Tsiang-lao</rt><rb>按杖</rb><rt>en-dziang</rt><rb>極力</rb><rt>gyih-lih</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪大拿</rb><rt>Mô-da-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪大拿</rb><rt>Mô-da-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿哈列</rb><rt>Nô-hah-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿哈列</rb><rt>Nô-hah-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴末</rb><rt>Pô-mah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴末</rb><rt>Pô-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟個</rb><rt>bing-yiang-go</rt><rb>嶴裏</rb><rt>ao-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比士迦</rb><rt>Pi-z-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-ting</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>對着</rb><rt>te-djôh</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打發</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>岔進</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>岔進</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>葡萄園裏</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>井裏個</rb><rt>tsing-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只用</rb><rt>tsih-yüong</rt><rb>大路</rb><rt>do-lu</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聚集</rb><rt>jü-jih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞哈斯</rb><rt>Üô-hah-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>打敗</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞泊</rb><rt>Üô-boh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞捫</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞捫</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>極其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希實本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>屬</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希實本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希實本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西宏</u>個</rb><rt>Si-ʽong-go</rt><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>從前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奪</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞捫</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>作過個</rb><rt>tsoh-ko-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希實本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希實本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>火焰</rb><rt>ho-yin</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>燒掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞珥</rb><rt>Üô-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>巴末</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-mah-go</rt><rb>諸侯</rb><rt>cü-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<u><ruby><rb>基抹</rb><rt>Kyi-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>敗掉</rb><rt>ba-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>擄掠去</rb><rt>lo-liah-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希實本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底本</rb><rt>Ti-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>毀滅</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>毀掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪法</rb><rt>Nô-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米底巴</rb><rt>Mi-ti-pô</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅設</rb><rt>Yüô-sih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞摩利</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>噩</rb><rt>Ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>率領</rb><rt>sah-ling</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以得來</rb><rt>Yi-teh-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>中</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>從前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>庉在</rb><rt>deng-dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希實本</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>中</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甚至於</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XXII.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>對着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西撥</rb><rt>Si-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>極其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>人數</rb><rt>nying-su</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>喫完</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-wun</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>草</rb><rt>tsʽao</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西撥</u>個</rb><rt>Si-pah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>河邊個</rb><rt>ʽO-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比奪</rb><rt>Pi-deh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>地面</rb><rt>di-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>強如</rb><rt>gyiang-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>得勝</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt><rb>打敗</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>祝福個</rb><rt>coh-foh-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>咒詛個</rb><rt>tsiu-cü-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>宿在</rb><rt>soh-dzæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>回報</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>ü-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西撥</u>個</rb><rt>Si-pah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>11</sup> 『<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>地面</rb><rt>di-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>祝福個</rb><rt>coh-foh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>本國</rb><rt>peng-koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起身</rb><rt>kyʽi-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>尊貴個</rb><rt>tseng-kwe-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西撥</rb><rt>Si-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>阻隔</rb><rt>tsu-kah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>頂大</rb><rt>ting-do</rt><rb>尊貴</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都會</rb><rt>tu-we</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>屋個</rb><rt>oh-go</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>少</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>宿</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>一夜</rb><rt>ih-yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>夜裏</rb><rt>yia-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若來</rb><rt>ziah-læ</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>備好</rb><rt>be-hao</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>大怒</rb><rt>da-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>騎勒</rb><rt>gyi-leh</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>寶劍</rb><rt>pao-tsin</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>岔進</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>葡萄園</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>中央個</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>都有</rb><rt>tu-yiu</rt><rb>牆</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>貼着</rb><rt>tʽiah-djôh</rt><rb>牆</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>軋住</rb><rt>gah-djü</rt><rb>牆</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走過去點</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>狹窄個</rb><rt>ʽah-tsah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>左邊</rb><rt>tso-pin</rt><rb>右邊</rb><rt>yiu-pin</rt><rb>嘸路</rb><rt>m-lu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>轉動</rb><rt>cün-dong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>睏倒</rb><rt>kwʽeng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>發怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>杖</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>驢子個</rb><rt>li-ts-go</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>輕慢</rb><rt>kyʽing-mæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>巴弗能夠</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寶劍</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>豈弗是</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>騎個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>從前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>過弗</rb><rt>ko-feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>寶劍</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>傴倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt><rb>撲在</rb><rt>pʽoh-dzæ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>走個</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>岔過</rb><rt>tsʽô-ko</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>岔過</rb><rt>tsʽô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>救活</rb><rt>kyiu-weh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倒走轉</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>Kʽo-yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>jün-r</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>邊界</rb><rt>pin-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河邊</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt><rb>屬</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>切身</rb><rt>tsʽih-sing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>豈弗能夠</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>尊貴</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>來東了</rb><rt>læ-tong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列胡朔</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-wu-soh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>獻祭</rb><rt>hyin-tsi</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來的個</rb><rt>læ-tih-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴力</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-lih-go</rt><rb>高地</rb><rt>kao-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>營寨個</rb><rt>ying-dza-go</rt><rb>外邊</rb><rt>nga-pin</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XXIII.

<u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>PÔ-LÆN</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>預備</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>壇上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>燒化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>會着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>光光</rb><rt>kwông-kwông</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>預備</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>每座</rb><rt>me-zo</rt><rb>壇上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>獻過</rb><rt>hyin-ko</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>燒化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌詞</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞蘭</rb><rt>Üô-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>東邊個</rb><rt>tong-pin-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>。』

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>咒詛個</rb><rt>tsiu-cü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>惡愫個</rb><rt>u-su-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>磐石上</rb><rt>bun-zah-zông</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>山頂上</rb><rt>sæn-ting-zông</rt><rb>望見</rb><rt>mông-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>獨自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>列國</rb><rt>lih-koh</rt><rb>算進</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>。
