<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>反</rb><rt>fæn</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>別</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>單</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大衆</rb><rt>da-cong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑣腓</rb><rt>So-fi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比士迦</rb><rt>Pi-z-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>壇上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>會着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>會着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介講</rb><rt>ka-kông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴勒</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講過</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌詞</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>起身</rb><rt>kyʽi-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西撥</rb><rt>Si-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>說謊</rb><rt>shih-hwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>悔改</rb><rt>hwe-kæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt><rb>豈弗會</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>講過</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算數</rb><rt>sön-su</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>反向</rb><rt>fæn-hyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>橫行不法</rb><rt>wang-ying-peh-fah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>呼和</rb><rt>hwu-ʽo</rt><rb>王個</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>剛強</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>野牛</rb><rt>yia-ngeo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>卜課</rb><rt>poh-kʽo</rt><rb>等事</rb><rt>teng-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>輪到</rb><rt>Leng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雌</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>獅子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跳起來</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>獅子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弶着個</rb><rt>gyiang-djôh-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>打煞個</rb><rt>tang-sah-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>睏倒</rb><rt>kwʽeng-tao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗用</rb><rt>feh-yüong</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗用</rb><rt>feh-yüong</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請來</rb><rt>Tsʽing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>別處</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>曠野個</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>預備</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>壇上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XXIV.

<u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>PÔ-LÆN</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>法術</rb><rt>fah-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>庉間</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌詞</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>比珥</u>個</rb><rt>Pi-r-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>Ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>閉的個</rb><rt>pi-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>全能</rb><rt>djün-neng</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>異像</rb><rt>yi-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撲倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>開的個</rb><rt>kʽæ-tih-go</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>5</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>庉處</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>華美</rb><rt>wô-me</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>佈開</rb><rt>pu-kʽæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>山嶴</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>河邊個</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-go</rt><rb>花園</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>種個</rb><rt>cong-go</rt><rb>沉香</rb><rt>dzing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>邊沿個</rb><rt>pin-yin-go</rt><rb>柏香</rb><rt>pah-hyiang</rt><rb>木</rb><rt>moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>溢出來</rb><rt>kah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>發生起來</rb><rt>fah-seng-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>高如</rb><rt>kao-jü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞甲</rb><rt>Üô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>剛強</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>猶之乎</rb><rt>yiu-ts-wu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>野牛</rb><rt>yia-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>吞喫</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>仇敵個</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>敲斷</rb><rt>kʽao-dön</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>骨頭</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>弓</rb><rt>kong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箭</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>射煞</rb><rt>zih-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>伏的</rb><rt>boh-tih</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>獅子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>睏倒</rb><rt>kwʽeng-tao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雌</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>獅子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>引動</rb><rt>ying-dong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>Coh-foh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt></ruby>。」』」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>發怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>括括</rb><rt>kweh-kweh</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>本鄉</rb><rt>peng-hyiang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>至於</rb><rt>ts-ü</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>尊貴</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>尊貴</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>過於</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌詞</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>Ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>閉的個</rb><rt>pi-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>知識</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>全能</rb><rt>djün-neng</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>異像個</rb><rt>yi-ziang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撲倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>開的個</rb><rt>kʽæ-tih-go</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>近來</rb><rt>gying-læ</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>皇上</rb><rt>wông-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>執</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>之</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>圭</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>興起</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>打過</rb><rt>tang-ko</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>兩邊</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>吵亂個</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>絲珥</rb><rt>S-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得勝</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄個</rb><rt>gyün-ping-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>剩落個</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」』」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌詞</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>列國個</rb><rt>lih-koh-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>終於</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>滅亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌詞</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>庉處</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>窠</rb><rt>kʽo</rt><rb>放在</rb><rt>fông-dzæ</rt><rb>磐石上</rb><rt>bun-zah-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基尼</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>衰敗</rb><rt>sæ-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞述</rb><rt>Üô-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>擄掠去</rb><rt>lo-liah-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歌詞</rb><rt>ko-dz</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>？

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基底</rb><rt>Kyi-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海邊</rb><rt>hæ-pin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞述</rb><rt>Üô-jih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯</rb><rt>Hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>本鄉</rb><rt>peng-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>巴勒</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XXV.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>失亭</rb><rt>Shih-ding</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女子</rb><rt>nyü-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>獻祭</rb><rt>hyin-tsi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力</rb><rt>Pô-lih</rt><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>發怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>對着</rb><rt>te-djôh</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吊煞</rb><rt>tiao-sah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>發個</rb><rt>fah-go</rt><rb>大怒</rb><rt>da-nu</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>止住</rb><rt>ts-djü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>士師</rb><rt>z-s</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力</rb><rt>Pô-lih</rt><rb>比珥</u>個</rb><rt>Pi-r-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟個</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞倫</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>孫子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以利亞撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>槍</rb><rt>tsʽiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>跟着</rb><rt>Keng-djôh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>戳煞</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>止住了</rb><rt>ts-djü-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>遭</rb><rt>Tsao</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二萬</rb><rt>nyi-væn</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>11</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>孫子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>息怒</rb><rt>sih-nu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嫉妒之心</rb><rt>dzih-tu-ts-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>dzih-tu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>平安個</rb><rt>bing-en-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>Pih-ding</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>祭司個</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嫉妒之心</rb><rt>dzih-tu-ts-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>殺個</rb><rt>sah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>心利</rb><rt>Sing-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒路</rb><rt>Sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西緬</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>族長</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>殺個</rb><rt>sah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥斯比</rb><rt>Ko-s-pi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>蘇珥</u>個</rb><rt>Su-r-go</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>奸計</rb><rt>kæn-kyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>誘惑</rb><rt>yiu-ʽoh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>牧伯個</rb><rt>moh-pah-go</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥斯比</rb><rt>Ko-s-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Ms. XXVI.

<ruby><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>W̆ENG-YÜOH</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>Kæ</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>廿歲</rb><rt>nyiæn-shü</rt><rb>以上</rb><rt>yi-zông</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>交戰個</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河邊</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>廿歲</rb><rt>nyiæn-shü</rt><rb>以上</rb><rt>yi-zông</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>長子</rb><rt>tsiang-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u>；<u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈諾</rb><rt>Ha-noh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈諾</rb><rt>Ha-noh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴路</rb><rt>Pô-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴路</rb><rt>Pô-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>Joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希斯崙</rb><rt>Hyi-s-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希斯崙</rb><rt>Hyi-s-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦米</rb><rt>Kyüô-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦米</rb><rt>Kyüô-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>派個</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四萬</rb><rt>s-væn</rt><rb>三千</rb><rt>sæn-tsʽin</rt><rb>七百</rb><rt>tsʽih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb><u>巴路</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-lu-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利押</rb><rt>Yi-li-iah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以利押</rb><rt>Yi-li-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼母利</rb><rt>Nyi-meo-li</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞比蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大丹</rb><rt>Da-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞比蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名望</rb><rt>ming-vông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同黨</rb><rt>dong-tông</rt><rb>叛逆</rb><rt>bun-nyih</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>亞倫</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此處原書錯印爲 Nyi-mo-li，後文皆爲 Nyi-meo-li</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>開口</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽeo</rt><rb>吞喫</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一黨</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-liao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>燒滅</rb><rt>siao-mih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哥拉</u>個</rb><rt>Ko-lah-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西緬</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>尼母利</u>個</rb><rt>Nyi-meo-li-go</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼母利</rb><rt>Nyi-meo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅憫</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-ming-go</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅憫</rb><rt>Yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅斤</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kying-go</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅斤</rb><rt>Yüô-kying</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>Joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西剌</u>個</rb><rt>Si-lah-go</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西剌</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>沙羅</u>個</rb><rt>Sô-lo-go</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>沙羅</rb><rt>Sô-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西緬</u>個</rb><rt>Si-min-go</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二萬</rb><rt>nyi-væn</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>洗分</rb><rt>Si-feng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>洗分</rb><rt>Si-feng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈基</rb><rt>Ha-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈基</rb><rt>Ha-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>書尼</rb><rt>Shü-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>書尼</rb><rt>Shü-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>Joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿斯尼</rb><rt>O-s-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿斯尼</rb><rt>O-s-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利</rb><rt>Yi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利</rb><rt>Yi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb><u>猶大</u>個</rb><rt>Yiu-da-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>珥</rb><rt>R</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿南</rb><rt>O-nen</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>珥</rb><rt>R</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿南</rb><rt>O-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法勒斯</rb><rt>Fah-lah-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法勒斯</rb><rt>Fah-lah-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希弗</rb><rt>Hyi-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西羅非哈</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>曷拉</rb><rt>ʽEh-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>得撒</rb><rt>Teh-sah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>派個</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五萬</rb><rt>ng-væn</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>七百</rb><rt>tsʽih-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>書提拉</rb><rt>Shü-di-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以蘭</rb><rt>Yi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以蘭</rb><rt>Yi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法蓮</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>派個</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三萬</rb><rt>sæn-væn</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>五百</rb><rt>ng-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比拉</rb><rt>Pi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比拉</rb><rt>Pi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞實別</rb><rt>Üô-jih-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞實別</rb><rt>Üô-jih-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞希蘭</rb><rt>Üô-hyi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞希蘭</rb><rt>Üô-hyi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>53</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>分撥</rb><rt>feng-peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名數</rb><rt>ming-su</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>60</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿答</rb><rt>Nô-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞比戶</rb><rt>üô-pi-wu</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以大瑪</rb><rt>Yi-da-mô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>63</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>派個</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶利哥</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河邊</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>64</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>派個</rb><rt>pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-leh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>個</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-lôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ms. XXVII.

<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>IAH-SEH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族個</rb><rt>dzoh-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>瑪吉</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-kyih-go</rt><rb>曾孫</rb><rt>tseng-seng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb><u>基列</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-go</rt><rb>孫子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>基弗</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-fah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西羅非哈</u>個</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha-go</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>曷拉</rb><rt>ʽEh-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>得撒</rb><rt>Teh-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>父親</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉</rb><rt>Ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一黨</rb><rt>ih-tông</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt></ruby>；

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>父親</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>請問</rb><rt>tsʽing-meng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>7</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>西羅非哈</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>父親個</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>本族個</rb><rt>peng-dzoh-go</rt><rb>親房</rb><rt>tsʽing-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>定例</rb><rt>ding-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞巴琳</rb><rt>Üô-pô-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>看過仔</rb><rt>Kʽen-ko-ts</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汛</u>個</rb><rt>Sing-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>爭鬥</rb><rt>tsang-teo</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汛</u>個</rb><rt>Sing-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>米利巴</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-li-pô-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。）」

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>性命個</rb><rt>sing-ming-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>靈個</rb><rt>ling-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>Kʽo-yi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>進出</rb><rt>tsing-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>免得</rb><rt>min-teh</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>羊牧個</rb><rt>yiang-moh-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>個</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>感動個</rb><rt>ken-dong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會個</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>尊貴</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>請問</rb><rt>tsʽing-meng</rt><rb>烏陵</rb><rt>U-ling</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定個</rb><rt>ding-go</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>進來</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立在</rb><rt>lih-dzæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>按手</rb><rt>en-siu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XXVIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>噴香個</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>火祭</rb><rt>ho-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>敬心</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定好個</rb><rt>ding-hao-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>火祭個</rb><rt>ho-tsi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>嘸毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>平常個</rb><rt>bing-djông-go</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>Ih-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>早上</rb><rt>tsao-zông</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晚上</rb><rt>mæn-zông</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>四股之一</rb><rt>s-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>榨出個</rb><rt>tsô-cʽih-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>；

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>平常個</rb><rt>bing-djông-go</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定個</rb><rt>ding-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>噴香</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang</rt><rb>獻撥</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>火祭</rb><rt>ho-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>獻</rb><rt>Hyin</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>奠酒</rb><rt>din-tsiu</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>四股</rb><rt>s-kwu</rt><rb>之一</rb><rt>ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>聖所</rb><rt>Sing-sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>燒酒</rb><rt>siao-tsiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>第二隻</rb><rt>Di-nyi-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晚上</rb><rt>mæn-zông</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奠酒</rb><rt>din-tsiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>早上</rb><rt>tsao-zông</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>噴香個</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>火祭</rb><rt>ho-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>En-sih-nyih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>嘸毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的</rb><rt>ʽo-tih</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>安息日個</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih-go</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>平常</rb><rt>bing-djông</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>奠酒</rb><rt>din-tsiu</rt><rb>之外個</rb><rt>ts-nga-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>月頭</rb><rt>Yüih-deo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>嘸毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>每隻</rb><rt>Me-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>噴香個</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>Sô</rt><rb>奠個</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt><rb>半欣</rb><rt>pun-hying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>一欣</rb><rt>ih-hying</rt><rb>四股之一</rb><rt>s-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>到頭</rb><rt>tao-deo</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>月個</rb><rt>yüih-go</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>平常</rb><rt>bing-djông</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>配攏</rb><rt>pʽe-long</rt><rb>奠個</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>Dzæ</rt><rb>正月</rb><rt>tsing-yüih</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嘸酵水</rb><rt>m-kao-shü</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>；

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>第一日</rb><rt>Di-ih-nyih</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聖會</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>苦工</rb><rt>kwʽu-kong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>嘸毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>配攏</rb><rt>pʽe-long</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭個</rb><rt>su-tsi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>：

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>早上個</rb><rt>tsao-zông-go</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>平常個</rb><rt>bing-djông-go</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>噴香個</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>平常個</rb><rt>bing-djông-go</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>奠個</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>第七日</rb><rt>Di-tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聖會</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>苦工</rb><rt>kwʽu-kong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>獻</rb><rt>Hyin</rt><rb>嘗新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>七七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>新個</rb><rt>sing-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聖會</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>苦工</rb><rt>kwʽu-kong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>噴香個</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-go</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>配攏個</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt></ruby>；

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>七隻</rb><rt>Tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>Ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>Djü</rt><rb>平常個</rb><rt>bing-djông-go</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>奠個</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>嘸</rb><rt>m</rt><rb>毛病</rb><rt>mao-bing</rt></ruby>。
