### Ms. XXIX.

<ruby><rb>七月</rb><rt>TSʽIH-YÜIH</rt><rb>初一</rb><rt>tsʽu-ih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聖會</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>苦工</rb><rt>kwʽu-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒個</rb><rt>ʽao-dong-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>香氣</rb><rt>hyiang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>嘸毛病個</rb><rt>m-mao-bing-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>配攏個</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt></ruby>；

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>七隻</rb><rt>Tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>定例</rb><rt>ding-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月頭</rb><rt>yüih-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>平常</rb><rt>bing-djông</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奠酒</rb><rt>din-tsiu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>香氣</rb><rt>hyiang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>七月</rb><rt>Tsʽih-yüih</rt><rb>初十</rb><rt>tsʽu-jih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聖會</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>苦苦</rb><rt>kwʽu-kwʽu</rt><rb>齋戒</rb><rt>tsæ-kyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>香氣</rb><rt>hyiang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>嘸毛病個</rb><rt>m-mao-bing-go</rt></ruby>；

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>配攏</rb><rt>pʽe-long</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>Ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>犯罪祭</rb><rt>væn-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>平常</rb><rt>bing-djông</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奠</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>之外個</rb><rt>ts-nga-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>七月</rb><rt>Tsʽih-yüih</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聖會</rb><rt>sing-we</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>苦工</rb><rt>kwʽu-kong</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>香氣</rb><rt>hyiang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>十三頭</rb><rt>jih-sæn-deo</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>十四隻</rb><rt>jih-s-tsah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>嘸毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>配攏個</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>十三隻</rb><rt>jih-sæn-tsah</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>十股之三</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>十股之二</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-r</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>十四隻</rb><rt>Jih-s-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>少年女</rb><rt>siao-nyin-nyü</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>父親個</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>許願</rb><rt>hyü-nyün</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>受戒</rb><rt>ziu-kyiæ</rt></ruby>；

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>父親</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing</rt><rb>一聽聞</rb><rt>ih-tʽing-meng</rt><rb>當時</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>許個</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt><rb>願</rb><rt>nyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>戒</rb><rt>kyiæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算數</rb><rt>sön-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>父親</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>禁住個</rb><rt>kying-djü-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>倘若</rb><rt>Tʽông-ziah</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>願心</rb><rt>nyün-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>受戒</rb><rt>ziu-kyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>許配</rb><rt>hyü-pʽe</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>；

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>開口</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽeo</rt><rb>止住</rb><rt>ts-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>許個</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt><rb>願</rb><rt>nyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>戒</rb><rt>kyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>許願</rb><rt>hyü-nyün</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算數</rb><rt>sön-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>忽略</rb><rt>hweh-liah</rt><rb>許個</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt><rb>願心</rb><rt>nyün-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>戒</rb><rt>kyiæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算數</rb><rt>sön-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>寡婦</rb><rt>kwô-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>離掉個</rb><rt>li-diao-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>許個</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt><rb>願</rb><rt>nyün</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>還個</rb><rt>wæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>戒</rb><rt>kyiæ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做成個</rb><rt>tso-dzing-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>許願</rb><rt>hyü-nyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受戒</rb><rt>ziu-kyiæ</rt></ruby>；

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>Dziang-fu</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>許個</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt><rb>願</rb><rt>nyün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>戒</rb><rt>kyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算數</rb><rt>sön-su</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>許個</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt><rb>願心</rb><rt>nyün-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>戒</rb><rt>kyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算數</rb><rt>sön-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赦免</rb><rt>sô-min</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>願心</rb><rt>nyün-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>戒個</rb><rt>kyiæ-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>算數</rb><rt>sön-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算數</rb><rt>sön-su</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>規條</rb><rt>kwe-diao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>論到</rb><rt>leng-tao</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>父親</rb><rt>vu-tsʽing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女兒個</rb><rt>nyü-r-go</rt><rb>倫理</rb><rt>leng-li</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XXXI.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>報仇</rb><rt>pao-dziu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>本族</rb><rt>peng-dzoh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>挑選</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>馱軍器個</rb><rt>do-kyüing-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>交戰個</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>報</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>全軍</rb><rt>djün-kyüing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>挑選</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>共數</rb><rt>gong-su</rt><rb>一萬</rb><rt>ih-væn</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>交戰個</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>一千個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以利亞撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>聖所個</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>太長聲個</rb><rt>tʽa-dziang-sing-go</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>帶勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此處原文錯印爲 Fi-ni-ha，前後文均爲 Fi-nyi-ha</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>男子</rb><rt>nen-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>在內個</rb><rt>dzæ-nen-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以未</rb><rt>Yi-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利金</rb><rt>Li-kying</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>蘇珥</rb><rt>Su-r</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>戶珥</rb><rt>Wu-r</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利巴</rb><rt>Li-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>比珥</u>個</rb><rt>Pi-r-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>寶劍</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>燒掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>營盤</rb><rt>ying-bun</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶來</rb><rt>ta-læ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>擄掠個</rb><rt>lo-liah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟個</rb><rt>bing-yiang-go</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河邊個</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公會個</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>營寨外</rb><rt>ying-dza-nga</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>帶領</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>千夫長</rb><rt>tsʽin-fu-tsiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百夫長</rb><rt>pah-fu-tsiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>發怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>救活</rb><rt>kyiu-weh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-læn-go</rt><rb>計較</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>比珥</u>個</rb><rt>Pi-r-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>故此</rb><rt>Kwu-tsʽ</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>男子</rb><rt>nen-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt><rb>過個</rb><rt>ko-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>同牀過</rb><rt>dong-zông-ko</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救活</rb><rt>kyiu-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>摸着</rb><rt>moh-djoh</rt><rb>屍首個</rb><rt>s-siu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>擄掠個</rb><rt>lo-liah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>每件</rb><rt>me-gyin</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>皮貨</rb><rt>bi-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>羊毛</rb><rt>yiang-mao</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>交戰個</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>律法個</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>規條</rb><rt>kwe-diao</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>金</rb><rt>Kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銀</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>鐵</rb><rt>tʽih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>錫</rb><rt>sih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>鉛</rb><rt>kʽæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>鍛鍊</rb><rt>tön-lin</rt></ruby>；

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>鍛鍊</rb><rt>tön-lin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>淨水</rb><rt>zing-shü</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>火裏</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>鍛鍊</rb><rt>tön-lin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公會個</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口個</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>擄來個</rb><rt>lo-læ-go</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>兩股</rb><rt>liang-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>交戰個</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>又要</rb><rt>Yi-iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>兵將</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>進貢個</rb><rt>tsing-kong-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>五百</rb><rt>ng-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>收來個</rb><rt>siu-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>搖祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>又要</rb><rt>Yi-iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>五十個</rb><rt>ng-jih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>收來個</rb><rt>siu-læ-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>聖所個</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>Djü</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>leh</rt><rb>兵將</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>奪來個</rb><rt>deh-læ-go</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六十七萬</rb><rt>loh-jih-tsʽih-væn</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>；

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>七萬</rb><rt>Tsʽih-væn</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>；

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>六萬</rb><rt>Loh-væn</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>匹</rb><rt>pʽih</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>三萬</rb><rt>Sæn-væn</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt><rb>過個</rb><rt>ko-go</rt><rb>女子</rb><rt>nyü-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>Ngeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三萬</rb><rt>sæn-væn</rt><rb>六千</rb><rt>loh-tsʽin</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>進貢</rb><rt>tsing-kong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七十二</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-nyi</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>Li-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三萬</rb><rt>sæn-væn</rt><rb>五百</rb><rt>ng-pah</rt><rb>匹</rb><rt>pʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>進貢</rb><rt>tsing-kong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六十一</rb><rt>loh-jih-ih</rt><rb>匹</rb><rt>pʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一萬</rb><rt>ih-væn</rt><rb>六千</rb><rt>loh-tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>進貢</rb><rt>tsing-kong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>二</rb><rt>nyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>進貢個</rb><rt>tsing-kong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>搖祭</rb><rt>yiao-tsi</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>交戰個</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt><rb>兵將</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>分開個</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>公會</rb><rt>Kong-we</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>三萬</rb><rt>sæn-væn</rt><rb>七千</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽin</rt><rb>五百</rb><rt>ng-pah</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>；

<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>三萬</rb><rt>Sæn-væn</rt><rb>六千</rb><rt>loh-tsʽin</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>三萬</rb><rt>Sæn-væn</rt><rb>五百</rb><rt>ng-pah</rt><rb>匹</rb><rt>pʽih</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>一萬</rb><rt>Ih-væn</rt><rb>六千</rb><rt>loh-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>47</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>無論</rb><rt>vu-leng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>聖所個</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>分個</rb><rt>feng-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>率領</rb><rt>Sah-ling</rt><rb>兵將個</rb><rt>ping-tsiang-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>千夫長</rb><rt>tsʽin-fu-tsiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百夫長</rb><rt>pah-fu-tsiang</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>派過</rb><rt>pʽa-ko</rt><rb>兵將個</rb><rt>ping-tsiang-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>金子個</rb><rt>kying-ts-go</rt><rb>首飾</rb><rt>siu-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手鐲</rb><rt>siu-gyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戒指</rb><rt>ka-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>環子</rb><rt>gwæn-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>項圈</rb><rt>ʽông-kyʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>金子個</rb><rt>kying-ts-go</rt><rb>首飾</rb><rt>siu-sih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>千夫長</rb><rt>Tsʽin-fu-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百夫長</rb><rt>pah-fu-tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>搖祭個</rb><rt>yiao-tsi-go</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>共數</rb><rt>gong-su</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一萬</rb><rt>ih-væn</rt><rb>六千</rb><rt>loh-tsʽin</rt><rb>七百</rb><rt>tsʽih-pah</rt><rb>五十個</rb><rt>ng-jih-go</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>兵將</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>擄來</rb><rt>lo-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>千夫長</rb><rt>tsʽin-fu-tsiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百夫長個</rb><rt>pah-fu-tsiang-go</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>帶進</rb><rt>ta-tsing</rt><rb>會幕裏</rb><rt>we-mo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. XXXII.

<ruby><rb><u>流便</u>個</rb><rt>LIU-BIN-GO</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦得</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅設</u>地</rb><rt>Yüô-sih-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>牲口個</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>流便</u>個</rb><rt>Liu-bin-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公會個</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>3</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>亞大錄</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>底本</rb><rt>Ti-peng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅設</rb><rt>Yüô-sih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>寧拉</rb><rt>Nying-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希實本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利亞利</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>示班</rb><rt>Z-pæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>尼破</rb><rt>Nyi-pʽo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比穩</rb><rt>Pi-w̆eng</rt></ruby></u>，

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>攻打個</rb><rt>kong-tang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>流便</u>個</rb><rt>Liu-bin-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>交戰個</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>還好</rb><rt>wa-hao</rt><rb>坐在</rb><rt>zo-dzæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>倒銳氣</rb><rt>tao-ze-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地嗎</rb><rt>di-ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>從前</rb><rt>Dzong-zin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt><rb>巴尼亞</rb><rt>Pô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>窺探</rb><rt>kwʽe-tʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以實各</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-jih-koh-go</rt><rb>嶴</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>倒銳氣</rb><rt>tao-ze-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>發怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>11</sup> 『<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出來個</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>廿歲</rb><rt>nyiæn-shü</rt><rb>以上個</rb><rt>yi-zông-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斷</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>跟從</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼洗</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶孚尼</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>個</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>跟從</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一代</rb><rt>ih-dæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>繼續</rb><rt>kyi-djôh</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>興起</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>發怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跟從</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又會</rb><rt>yi-we</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>羊圈</rb><rt>yiang-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>軍器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>堅固個</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免得</rb><rt>min-teh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>土人</rb><rt>tʽu-nying</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>朝東個</rb><rt>dziao-tong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>軍器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>兵將個</rb><rt>ping-tsiang-go</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>介做</rb><rt>ka-tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>況且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>羊圈</rb><rt>yiang-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講過個</rb><rt>kông-ko-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>流便</u>個</rb><rt>Liu-bin-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊隊</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>庉在</rb><rt>deng-dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基列</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>軍器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>交戰個</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>關着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>個</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>迦得</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>流便</u>個</rb><rt>Liu-bin-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>軍器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kyiao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前個</rb><rt>min-zin-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>軍器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>軍器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西宏</u>個</rb><rt>Si-ʽong-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>噩</u>個</rb><rt>Ngoh-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>地界個</rb><rt>di-ka-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底本</rb><rt>Ti-peng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞大錄</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞羅珥</rb><rt>Üô-lo-r</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>押律</rb><rt>Iah-lih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>說反</rb><rt>Shih-fæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅設</rb><rt>Yüô-sih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>約比哈</rb><rt>Iah-pi-ha</rt></ruby></u>；

<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>伯寧拉</rb><rt>Pah-nying-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯哈蘭</rb><rt>Pah-ha-læn</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>造圈</rb><rt>zao-gyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希實本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利亞利</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基列亭</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-ding</rt></ruby></u>；

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼破</rb><rt>Nyi-pʽo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴力免</rb><rt>Pô-lih-min</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>悉瑪</rb><rt>Sih-mô</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>換過</rb><rt>wun-ko</rt></ruby>）；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>建造個</rb><rt>kyin-zao-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb><u>瑪拿西</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>瑪吉</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-kyih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>瑪拿西</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪吉</rb><rt>Mô-kyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb><u>瑪拿西</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>睚珥</rb><rt>Yiæ-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>稱</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>睚珥</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-r-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪巴</rb><rt>Nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基納</rb><rt>Kyi-nah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>屬</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基納</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-nah-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>稱</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪巴</rb><rt>Nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>。

### Ms. XXXIII.

<ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH-GO</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞倫</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>隊兵</rb><rt>de-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>記錄</rb><rt>kyi-loh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>正月</rb><rt>Tsing-yüih</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蘭塞</rb><rt>Læn-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>後</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>第二日</rb><rt>di-nyi-nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>舞腳</rb><rt>vu-kyiah</rt><rb>撩手</rb><rt>liao-siu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>殺掉過個</rb><rt>sah-diao-ko-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>頭生個</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>征伐</rb><rt>tsing-vah</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蘭塞</rb><rt>Læn-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>搭營寨</rb><rt>tah-ying-dza</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>疏割</rb><rt>Su-keh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>疏割</rb><rt>Su-keh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>邊沿個</rb><rt>pin-yin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以藤</rb><rt>Yi-deng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以藤</rb><rt>Yi-deng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力洗分</rb><rt>Pô-lih-si-feng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相對個</rb><rt>siang-te-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比哈希錄</rb><rt>Pi-ha-hyi-loh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>密奪</rb><rt>Mih-dah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比哈希錄</rb><rt>Pi-ha-hyi-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以藤</rb><rt>Yi-deng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>邊</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯耶西末</rb><rt>Pah-yiæ-si-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯士亭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-z-ding</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>51</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>Pih-iao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>雕刻</rb><rt>tiao-kʽeh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>鑄出個</rb><rt>cü-cʽih-go</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拆毀</rb><rt>tsʽah-hwe</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>高地上個</rb><rt>kao-di-zông-go</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉在</rb><rt>deng-dzæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>指末頭</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>多個</rb><rt>to-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>缺個</rb><rt>kyʽüih-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撮出</rb><rt>tsʽah-cʽih</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>指末頭</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>箇地個</rb><rt>keh-di-go</rt><rb>土人</rb><rt>tʽu-nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>剩落個</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-go</rt><rb>土人</rb><rt>tʽu-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腰脅裏</rb><rt>iao-hyih-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>註定</rb><rt>cü-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>降災</rb><rt>kyüông-tsæ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>土人</rb><rt>tʽu-nying</rt><rb>反向</rb><rt>fæn-hyiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。』」

---

### Ms. XXXIV.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業個</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>南邊個</rb><rt>Nen-pin-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是從</rb><rt>z-dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汛</u>個</rb><rt>Sing-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>鹽海個</rb><rt>Yin-hæ-go</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>朝東</rb><rt>dziao-tong</rt><rb>爲止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>繞轉</rb><rt>nyiao-cün</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞克拉濱</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-kʽah-lah-ping-go</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汛</rb><rt>Sing</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵巴尼亞</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih-Pô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈薩</rb><rt>Ha-sah</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>亞達</rb><rt>Üô-dah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>押們</rb><rt>Iah-meng</rt></ruby></u>；

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>押們</rb><rt>Iah-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>轉彎</rb><rt>cün-w̆æn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>爲止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>西邊</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt><rb>以</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大 海</rb><rt>da-hæ</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>界</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>西邊</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>北邊</rb><rt>Poh-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>轉</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>；

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何珥</rb><rt>ʽO-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>轉</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈末</rb><rt>Ha-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西達</rb><rt>Si-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>；

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>Di-ka</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>錫分</rb><rt>Sih-feng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈薩以難</rb><rt>Ha-sah-yi-næn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>北邊</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈薩以難</rb><rt>Ha-sah-yi-næn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示番</rb><rt>Z-fæn</rt></ruby></u>；

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示番</rb><rt>Z-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>延到</rb><rt>yin-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>立拉</rb><rt>Lih-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>繞轉</rb><rt>nyiao-cün</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞延</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-yin-go</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又要</rb><rt>yi-iao</rt><rb>延到</rb><rt>yin-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼烈</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>之</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>Di-ka</rt><rb>又要</rb><rt>yi-iao</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>lôh-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>鹽海</rb><rt>Yin-hæ</rt><rb>爲止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末頭</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業個</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>九個</rb><rt>kyiu-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby>：

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>出起</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>Dzing-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>貨物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</u>個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。
