<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>Dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>派定</rb><rt>pʽa-ding</rt><rb>三座</rb><rt>sæn-zo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>三座</rb><rt>sæn-zo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>逃命</rb><rt>Dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>外來</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt><rb>其中個</rb><rt>gyi-cong-go</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>無意</rb><rt>vu-i</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鐵個</rb><rt>tʽih-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>打人</rb><rt>tang-nying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>木器</rb><rt>moh-kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>報仇個</rb><rt>Pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怨氣</rb><rt>ün-kyʽi</rt><rb>推其</rb><rt>tʽe-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>埋伏間</rb><rt>ma-voh-kæn</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怨</rb><rt>ün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打個</rb><rt>tang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>報仇個</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>一碰着</rb><rt>ih-pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>除其</rb><rt>djü-gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>怨仇</rb><rt>ün-dziu</rt><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>推其</rb><rt>tʽe-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>埋伏個</rb><rt>ma-voh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>ang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>偶湊</rb><rt>neo-tsʽeo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>ang</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>怨仇</rb><rt>ün-dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>有意</rb><rt>yiu-i</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>：

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>打個</rb><rt>tang-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>報仇個</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>公會</rb><rt>Kong-we</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>報仇個</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>躲避過個</rb><rt>to-bi-ko-go</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅過個</rb><rt>fu-ko-go</rt><rb>祭司長</rb><rt>tsi-s-tsiang</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>；

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>況且</rb><rt>Hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>報仇個</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>祭司長</rb><rt>tsi-s-tsiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>祭司長</rb><rt>tsi-s-tsiang</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>以上</rb><rt>Yi-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>載的個</rb><rt>tsæ-tih-go</rt><rb>規條</rb><rt>kwe-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>審判個</rb><rt>sing-pʽun-go</rt><rb>定例</rb><rt>ding-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩三個</rb><rt>liang-sæn-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證個</rb><rt>te-tsing-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>死罪個</rb><rt>si-ze-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>躲</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城個</rb><rt>dzing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司長</rb><rt>tsi-s-tsiang</rt><rb>未死</rb><rt>vi-si</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>本鄉</rb><rt>peng-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>血個</rb><rt>hyüih-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只好</rb><rt>tsih-hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>斷</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>住在</rb><rt>djü-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」

### Ms. XXXVI.

<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>IAH-SEH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>孫子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>瑪吉</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-kyih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>族長</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>族長</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>指末頭</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt><rb>分撥</rb><rt>feng-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西羅非哈</u>個</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>減落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>減落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>禧年</rb><rt>hyi-nyin</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>減落個</rb><rt>kæn-lôh-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>屬個</rb><rt>joh-go</rt><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>減落</rb><rt>kæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西羅非哈</u>個</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha-go</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>Kʽo-yi</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>只要</rb><rt>tsih-iao</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>移</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>無論</rb><rt>vu-leng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>移過</rb><rt>yi-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb><u>西羅非哈</u>個</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha-go</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb><u>西羅非哈</u>個</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha-go</rt><rb>女兒</rb><rt>nyü-r</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>得撒</rb><rt>Tah-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>曷拉</rb><rt>ʽEh-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>叔伯個</rb><rt>soh-pah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>瑪拿西</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>以上</rb><rt>Yi-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>載的個</rb><rt>tsæ-tih-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>。
